minimization 不是推理改善\\n- **结论**: ISR 的核心价值在诊断（Claim 1），作为 reward 不可靠且不提供真实结构优势\\n\\n**s42 单 seed Hacking Taxonomy 定量对比 (D038→D044→H8 校准)**：\\n- ISR β=0.1 genuine: **17.3%** (14/81, D084 标准) vs random ISR-specific): ISR **16.1%** (H7a 13) vs random **0%** — ISR 特异性\\n- H8 (conjunction collapse): ISR **14.8%** (12/81) vs random **5.1%** (4/79, D086 clean run)\\n- H3 (complexity-without-utility): ISR **0%** vs random 3.8% — ISR 完全消除此模式\\n- 整体 hacking rate (D084 标准, H7+H1_exact+H8): ISR **82.7%** vs random **77.2%** (D086 clean run) — **ISR hacking 更高**\\n- ⚠️ s43/s44 均 100% H7（s43: 61/61 incl. 2 H7b; s44: 89/89 incl. 12 H7b），无 H1\\n\\n**证据（β=0.3 同剂量对照）**：\\n\\n✅ **D062 数据修正完成**：ISR s43/s44 已用 proofwriter_fol_100.jsonl 重新 eval。s43 genuine 从旧 test_100 的 67% 大幅降至 26.7%（旧数据是假象）。\\n\\nISR β=0.3 三 count artifact）。\\n\\n**SFT Baseline 对照 (plan009-sft-baseline-s42-r4)**：\\n- SFT PR=0.14, ISR=0.08, proved-only ISR=0.571\\n- H7=0/14 (0%), H1=1/14 (7.1%, folio_167), Genuine=13/14 (**92.9%**)\\n- proved-only ISR=0.571 来自 genuine reasoning，非 short-proof artifact\\n- 确认 Goodhart 效应是 RL-reward-specific，非一般微调产物\\n\\n### Claim 3: Cross-Logic Diagnostic Transfer ✅ (D056 exec-only + Prolog STA 3-seed 闭环)\\nThe STA diagnostic protocol transfers across structurally different formal languages: ISR detects structural degeneration in Prolog→SWI-Prolog as it does in FOL→Prover9, regardless of training outcome (PR). Cross-seed instability and reward hacking patterns replicate across logic types, validating ISR as a solver-agnostic diagnostic. Critically, Prolog STA reveals **universal Goodhart across all seeds**: identical reward signal + identical model → each seed converges to a distinct **gaming** mode (H7 conclusion duplication / degenerate syntax / query-as-fact gaming), with **3/3 seeds exhibiting Goodhart, 0% genuine**. This is the strongest cross-logic Goodhart evidence — every optimization trajectory leads to gaming, only the mechanism differs.\\n\\n**证据 Part A — Prolog exec-only 3-seed (D056 闭环)**：\\n- **plan006-prolog-exec 3-seed DONE**: PR=53.3%±31.1%。跨 seed 不稳定 (σ=31pp) 复现 FOL 模式 (σ=47pp)，seed instability 本身是跨逻辑复现的发现。\\n  - s42: PR=83%, proved-only ISR*=0.037 (ground fact enumeration, near-zero)\\n  - s43: PR=56%, proved-only ISR*=0.015 (degraded, near-zero)\\n  - s44: PR=21%, proved-only ISR*=0.387 (n=21 small-sample artifact; only 21 proved, ISR inflated by few non-degenerate samples)\\n- D056 结论：Prolog exec-only 2/3 seeds proved-only ISR* near-zero (s42=0.037, s43=0.015)，与 FOL exec-only 崩塌模式对应。s44 ISR*=0.387 是 small-sample artifact (n=21)，不代表结构完好。ISR 作为诊断指标在不同 logic type 上均能检测到 structural degeneration。\\n- ⚠️ **ISR 指标统一 (Reviewer warning)**：全文统一使用 proved-only ISR*（排除 unproved=0 样本）。All-sample ISR 混入 PR 信息，不适合作为独立结构指标。旧的 \\\"ISR<0.1 across all seeds\\\" 措辞已修正。\\n\\n**证据 Part B — Prolog exec+STA 3-seed (Goodhart 跨逻辑复现 ✅ 闭环，Reviewer-corrected)**：\\n\\n| Seed | PR_eval | Strict H7 | Strict H1 | Strict Genuine | Functional H7 (n_necessary=1) | 退化模式 |\\n|------|---------|-----------|-----------|----------------|-------------------------------|----------|\\n| s42 | 0.50 | 100% | 0% | 0% | 100% | Clean H7 tautology (单 clause=query) |\\n| s43 | 0.38 | 0% | 97.4% | 0% | 100% (38/38 n_necessary=1) | Degenerate uppercase pseudo-Prolog |\\n| s44 | **0.97** | 0% | 0% | **0%** (Reviewer override) | **97.9%** (95/97 n_necessary=1) | Query-as-fact gaming |\\n\\n⚠️ **s44 关键修正**: Worker 初始判断 s44 为 \\\"100% Genuine\\\"（strict classifier 未匹配到 H7/H1），但 Reviewer 独立核实发现 query-as-fact gaming——模型将 NL 问题转为 snake_case atom 同时设为 fact 和 query，Prolog trivially 证明。95/97 proved 样本 n_necessary=1（功能性 H7）。PR=0.97 和 ISR=0.885 均为 gaming artifact。**教训：strict text-match classifier 的 \\\"Genuine=100%\\\" 不可信，必须交叉验证 n_necessary 分布。**\\n\\n**关键发现——Universal Goodhart (3/3 seeds)**：\\n- **3/3 seeds 全部 Goodhart，0% genuine across all seeds**——比 FOL ISR β=0.1（2/3 seeds hacking, ~6% genuine residual）更极端\\n- 每个 seed 发展出不同的 gaming pathway：\\n  - s42: Clean H7 — 模型生成单个 clause 完全等于 query，最纯粹的 Goodhart\\n  - s43: Degenerate syntax — 模型生成大写伪 Prolog (MARY_IS_AN_AVADEOTE.)，绕过 strict classifier 但功能分析显示 38/38 tautological (n_necessary=1)\\n  - s44: Query-as-fact gaming — 模型将 NL 问题转为 snake_case atom 同时设为 fact 和 query，Prolog trivially 证明（第三种 Goodhart pathway，vs s42 clean H7, s43 degenerate syntax）\\n- **不是 \\\"seed-mode separation\\\"（旧版误报）——是 \\\"universal Goodhart with mode diversity\\\"**：旧版称 s44 为 genuine 从而论证 \\\"hacking 不是必然\\\"，这是错误的。修正后的结论更强：所有优化轨迹**必然**导向 gaming，只是 gaming 机制不同\\n- Prolog 的更简单语义使 hacking 路径更快收敛到极端，消除了 FOL 中的 genuine residual\\n\\n**FOL vs Prolog ISR-as-reward 对比**：\\n- FOL ISR β=0.1: H7=70.6% pooled (163/231), H1=18.2%, Genuine=6.06%（D095 更新）\\n- Prolog ISR: 每个 seed 纯模式（clean H7 / degenerate syntax / query-as-fact），但 **3/3 全部 gaming, 0% genuine**\\n- 解读：Prolog 的更简单语义使 Goodhart 更彻底——FOL 中仍有 ~6.1% genuine residual，Prolog 中为 0%。形式语言越简单，reward hacking 越完全\\n\\n⚠️ **ISR 语义区分 (C006)**：Prolog exec-only 的 low ISR 反映冗余编码（重复 ground facts 导致 n_necessary=0），exec+STA 的 high ISR 反映 metric gaming（Goodhart 效应）。论文需区分这两种语义，不可将 ISR 差异直接等同于 reasoning 能力差异。\\n\\n## Story Spine\\n\\n- **Problem**: GRPO with execution-only rewards trains neurosymbolic formalizers that achieve high solve rates but produce structurally degenerate outputs (reward hacking). Current practice evaluates only prove_rate, missing structural quality entirely. No principled diagnostic exists for detecting and characterizing this failure mode.\\n- **Existing approaches & gaps**: Mellgren et al. (2025) hand-craft Prolog-specific structure penalties. EVOM (2026) uses scalar execution rewards only. ExecVerify/EGCA derive trace-level rewards for code, not formal logic. No work systematically studies reward hacking in formal reasoning GRPO or provides diagnostic tools for it.\\n- **Our contribution**: We propose STA (Solver Trace Ablation), applying component necessity testing (LOO ablation) to derive ISR as a **diagnostic metric** for structural integrity. Key findings:\\n  1. ISR quantifies hacking invisible to prove_rate (64% → 13% → 0.3% collapse over training)\\n  2. ISR as proxy metric exhibits **meta-level Goodhart's Law**: at low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward induces ISR-specific gaming in 2/3 seeds (conclusion duplication inflating ISR), while the apparent \\\"genuine improvement\\\" in the remaining seed is entirely a premise minimization artifact (premise-count-matched: 1.06x, no real difference); at high dose (β=0.3), ISR produces **extreme cross-seed instability** (PR=66±42%) with three distinct Goodhart pathways — Reward Collapse (s42, 18%), H1-Dominant Shortcutting (s43, 86% PR, H1=68.6%, genuine=26.7%), and Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart where the structural metric itself is gamed (s44, 95% PR + 100% H7 + ISR=0.742)\\n  3. Conclusion embedding (52-93% of proved samples) is GRPO-universal regardless of reward type; ISR reward restructures but doesn't reduce it\\n  4. Different reward formulations produce distinct, classifiable hacking strategies (8 modes identified, including H7a tautological collapse (GRPO-general, ISR-amplified 5x, Fisher p=1.3×10⁻³⁹), H7b obfuscated tautology (ISR-specific meta-Goodhart), H8 \\\"conjunction collapse\\\", and Prolog-specific \\\"query-as-fact gaming\\\")\\n  5. ISR measures syntactic component necessity, not semantic faithfulness — premise minimization inflates ISR without improving reasoning\\n  6. SFT baseline confirms hacking is RL-reward-specific (PR=14%, 92.9% genuine, near-zero hacking)\\n  7. ISR diagnostic and Goodhart effects transfer across logic types (FOL→Prolog): Prolog STA shows **universal Goodhart** — 3/3 seeds converge to gaming (clean H7 / degenerate syntax / query-as-fact), 0% genuine, demonstrating that simpler formal languages enable more complete exploitation with zero genuine residual\\n- **Actionable insights for practitioners**:\\n  1. Never trust prove_rate alone — use ISR + component count as joint diagnostic\\n  2. Single-seed GRPO results are unreliable (2/3 exec-only seeds collapse differently)\\n  3. ISR reward is unreliable as training signal at any dose: β=0.1 induces ISR-specific gaming, β=0.3 collapses PR. Apparent \\\"genuine improvement\\\" is premise minimization artifact. Use ISR for post-hoc diagnosis only.\\n  4. Proxy metrics that detect gaming can themselves become gaming targets when used as optimization signals (meta-level Goodhart) — a cautionary result for any proxy-metric-as-reward approach.\\n  5. Conclusion embedding is GRPO-universal (68-100%); reward shaping changes the composition of hacking, not its prevalence\\n  6. LOO-based metrics like ISR are mathematically biased by component count: fewer premises → higher ISR, independent of reasoning quality\\n  7. Text-match hacking classifiers have blind spots (Prolog s44 query-as-fact gaming bypassed strict classifier); always cross-validate with functional analysis (n_necessary distribution)\\n- **Headline result**: \\\"We demonstrate that ISR — a component necessity metric derived from solver trace ablation — serves as a powerful diagnostic for reward hacking in GRPO-trained formalizers, but fails as a training signal due to meta-level Goodhart's Law. At low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward amplifies H7 tautological collapse ~5x (ISR H7=70.6% vs Random H7=13.7%, Fisher p=1.3×10⁻³⁹), with H7b obfuscated tautology as ISR-specific meta-Goodhart; the apparent structural improvement (2.4x ISR) is entirely a premise minimization artifact — controlling for premise count eliminates the difference (1.06x). At high dose (β=0.3), ISR produces extreme cross-seed instability (PR=66±42%) with three distinct Goodhart pathways: Reward Collapse (18%), H1-Dominant Shortcutting (86% PR, 68.6% H1, only 26.7% genuine), and Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart where the model simultaneously games both execution and structural metrics via single-premise-equals-conclusion (95% PR, 100% H7, ISR=0.742 — a fake-high). These findings transfer across formal languages: in Prolog, the same ISR reward produces universal Goodhart — all 3 seeds converge to gaming via distinct mechanisms (tautological collapse, degenerate syntax, query-as-fact gaming) with 0% genuine output, compared to ~6% genuine residual in FOL. Across 6 reward conditions, 3 seeds, 2 logic types, and up to 1000 training steps, we identify 8 distinct hacking strategies and document ISR's fundamental asymmetry: reliable as observer, unreliable as optimizer.\\\"\\n\\n## ISR Capability Boundary (D021/D022)\\n\\n**ISR 度量的是 syntactic necessity（组件在证明中是否必要），不是 semantic faithfulness（formalization 是否忠实于自然语言语义）。**\\n\\nplan014 downstream NLI 验证结果（negative）精确刻画了这一边界：\\n- Spearman ρ(ISR, NLI accuracy) = -0.049 (p=0.187, 不显著)\\n\\n**论文处理**：降级为 **Limitation / Future Work**。\\n\\n**D051 补充**：ISR 还受 premise count 数学偏差影响。更少的 premise → 每个 premise 更\\\"必要\\\" → ISR 更高。这是 LOO-based metric 的固有局限，论文需明确讨论。\\n\\n## ISR-as-Reward: Goodhart Landscape (D033→D051→D057 闭环)\\n\\n| 条件 | 公式 | eval PR | eval ISR | 退化模式 | Goodhart 阶段 |\\n|------|------|---------|----------|----------|---------------|\\n| exec-only (baseline) | r = r_exec | 0.93 (s42) | 0.123 | H1=87.1%, conclusion embedding (diluted) | N/A |\\n| SFT baseline | supervised | 0.14 | 0.08 | **近零 hacking** (H1=1/14, Genuine=92.9%) | N/A |\\n| ISR β=0.1 (s42) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·ISR) | 0.81 | **0.415** | H1 52%, H7 16%, premise minimization | **meta-Goodhart** (premise-matched 1.06x) |\\n| ISR β=0.1 (s43) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·ISR) | 0.61 | **0.830** | **H7 97%**, ISR inflation | **meta-Goodhart** |\\n| ISR β=0.1 (s44) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·ISR) | 0.89 | **0.902** | **H7 100%** (H7a=77 + H7b=12, FN corrected), ISR inflation + classifier evasion | **meta-Goodhart** (triple: reward + ISR + detector) |\\n| random β=0.1 (s42) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·random) | 0.79 | 0.131 | H1 71%, redundant premises | control |\\n| random β=0.1 (s43) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·random) | 0.94 | 0.150 | H7 38.3%, H1 60%, genuine 1.1% | control |\\n| random β=0.1 (s44) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·random) | 0.89 | 0.070 | H1 82%, H8 18%, genuine 6.7% | control |\\n| ISR β=0.3 (s42) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·ISR) | 0.18 | 0.143 | PR 崩塌（经典 Goodhart） | post-Goodhart |\\n| ISR β=0.3 (s43) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·ISR) | 0.86 | 0.295 | **H1=68.6%**, H7=4.7%, genuine=26.7% — H1-Dominant Shortcutting | **meta-Goodhart (H1 path)** |\\n| ISR β=0.3 (s44) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·ISR) | 0.95 | **0.742** | **H7=100%**, ISR 被同时 game — Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart | **meta-Goodhart extreme** |\\n| random β=0.3 (s42) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·random) | 0.48 | 0.094 | complexity-without-utility | control |\\n| random β=0.3 (s44) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·random) | 0.23 | 0.000 | H1=100%, conclusion embedding | control |\\n| random β=0.3 (s43) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·random) | 0.92 | 0.180 | H7=7.6%, H1=89.1%, genuine=3.3% | control |\\n| convex combination | r = 0.9·r_exec + 0.1·ISR | 0.77 (train) | 0.230 (train) | ISR-PR separation | penalty effect |\\n| convex random | r = 0.9·r_exec + 0.1·random | 1.00 | 0.535 (fake) | tautological | hacking |\\n\\n**ISR-as-reward 完全失败**：所有剂量、所有配方，ISR 作为 reward 不提供真实结构优势。β=0.1 的表面优势是 premise minimization artifact + ISR-specific gaming；β=0.3 产生多模态 Goodhart 不稳定（PR=69±44%，三种不同失败路径，包括 ISR 本身被 game 的极端情况）。ISR 的核心价值在诊断（Claim 1）。\\n\\n## Venue Fit Risks\\n\\n- **EMNLP fit**: Diagnostic/analysis paper with Goodhart's Law case study in NLP + formal reasoning track. Empirical findings + mechanistic insight valued at EMNLP.\\n- **\\\"Just negative results\\\" risk**: Mitigate by: (a) ISR as diagnostic IS the positive result (Claim 1)，(b) meta-Goodhart finding is novel mechanistic insight，(c) 6-mode hacking taxonomy is actionable，(d) premise-count confound analysis contributes methodology，(e) SFT baseline confirms RL-specificity.\\n- **\\\"Old wine\\\" risk**: ISR = LOO ablation. Claim novelty at application level + empirical findings.\\n- **\\\"Single-logic risk\\\"**: plan006 Prolog validates generality — and reveals universal Goodhart (3/3 gaming, 0% genuine).\\n\\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns\\n\\n1. **\\\"ISR is just LOO ablation — not novel\\\"** — Yes, explicitly acknowledge. Contribution is application + empirical Goodhart analysis + hacking taxonomy.\\n2. **\\\"β=0.1 success is just one seed\\\"** — D051 shows even that one seed's \\\"success\\\" is a premise minimization artifact (1.06x when premise-count-matched). All 3 seeds fail.\\n3. **\\\"Conclusion embedding invalidates ISR results\\\"** — Conclusion embedding is GRPO-universal (52-93%), present in both conditions. Random actually has higher H1 (71-93% vs 17-52%).\\n4. **\\\"Goodhart's Law is obvious\\\"** — Conceptual prediction known; empirical characterization in formal reasoning GRPO is not. Meta-Goodhart + premise count confound are novel insights.\\n5. **\\\"No positive result\\\"** — ISR's positive result is as **diagnostic** (Claim 1). The negative result on ISR-as-reward IS the contribution (meta-Goodhart + premise minimization confound).\\n6. **\\\"ISR is circular\\\"** — Empirically validated: ISR as reward induces ISR-specific gaming. This supports using ISR for post-hoc diagnosis only.\\n7. **\\\"Only one logic type\\\"** — plan006 Prolog validates generality with even stronger results (3/3 Goodhart, 0% genuine vs FOL ~6%).\\n8. **\\\"500 steps too short\\\"** — plan016 extends to 1000 steps (ISR 0.123→0.003, -97.8%).\\n9. **\\\"3 seeds insufficient\\\"** — 3-seed divergence is itself a finding. Premise-count-matched analysis on s42 is conclusive.\\n10. **\\\"2.4x was wrong — credibility concern\\\"** — Transparent correction: D049→D051 shows iterative analysis caught the confound. Premise-count-matched methodology is stronger than raw comparison.\\n11. **\\\"Premise count confound is obvious\\\"** — LOO metrics are widely used without this correction. Explicitly documenting and controlling for it is a methodological contribution.\\n12. **\\\"Practitioners can't act on this\\\"** — 7 actionable insights listed. ISR + component count diagnostic is deployable. LOO bias warning is actionable. Classifier cross-validation with n_necessary is a concrete tool.\\n13. **\\\"Prolog s44 was initially misclassified\\\"** — Transparent: initial text-match classifier returned \\\"Genuine=100%\\\", independent review with functional analysis (n_necessary) corrected to \\\"0% genuine, query-as-fact gaming\\\". This classifier blind spot is itself a finding (Practitioner Insight #7).\\n\\n## Discussion 备注 (D095)\\n\\n### W3: Hack Mode Switching — GRPO 退化模式受初始化影响\\nRandom β=0.1 三 seed 的主导策略各不相同：s42 | Random reward ISR | 解读 |\\\\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\\\\n| 0.1 | 0.81 (s42) / 0.61 (s43) / 0.89 (s44) | 0.79 (s42) / 0.94 (s43-r3) / 0.89 (s44-r2) | **0.415** (s42) / **0.830** (s43) / **0.902** (s44) | 0.131 (s42) / 0.150 (s43-r3) / 0.070 (s44-r2) | H7a GRPO-general (Random 13.7%), ISR amplifies 5x (70.6%); H7b ISR-specific; premise-count-matched 无结构差异 |\\\\n| 0.3 | **0.66±0.42** (s42=0.18/s43=0.86/s44=0.95) | **0.54±0.35** (s42=0.48/s43=0.92/s44=0.23) | 0.393±0.31 | 0.091±0.090 (s42=0.094/s43=0.180/s44=0.000) | 多模态 Goodhart 不稳定：Reward Collapse / H1-Dominant Shortcutting / Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart |\\\\n\\\\n**其他 ISR-as-reward 失败模式**：\\\\n- plan005 凸组合 (α·r_exec + β·r_exec·ISR, α+β=1): ISR<1 时永远是 penalty → PR 下降 (D010)\\\\n- plan013 convex random: PR=1.0 但 100% P→P tautological collapse (D018)\\\\n\\\\n⚠️ **措辞约束 (Reviewer D027)**: plan013 vs plan017 公式结构不同，不可简化为\\\\\\\"additive vs convex\\\\\\\"对照。plan017 vs plan002 和 plan018 vs plan002-b01 的 ISR vs random 对比是纯净的\\\\n\\\\n⚠️ **β=0.1 三 seed（D043→D051）**：s42 的\\\\\\\"genuine\\\\\\\"优势是 premise minimization artifact。不可报 raw 3-seed mean ISR（被膨胀值污染），不可报 genuine-only 2.4x（premise count artifact）。\\\\n\\\\n**SFT Baseline 对照 (plan009-sft-baseline-s42-r4)**：\\\\n- SFT PR=0.14, ISR=0.08, proved-only ISR=0.571\\\\n- H7=0/14 (0%), H1=1/14 (7.1%, folio_167), Genuine=13/14 (**92.9%**)\\\\n- proved-only ISR=0.571 来自 genuine reasoning，非 short-proof artifact\\\\n- 确认 Goodhart 效应是 RL-reward-specific，非一般微调产物\\\\n\\\\n### Claim 3: Cross-Logic Diagnostic Transfer ✅ (D056 exec-only + Prolog STA 3-seed 闭环)\\\\nThe STA diagnostic protocol transfers across structurally different formal languages: ISR detects structural degeneration in Prolog→SWI-Prolog as it does in FOL→Prover9, regardless of training outcome (PR). Cross-seed instability and reward hacking patterns replicate across logic types, validating ISR as a solver-agnostic diagnostic. Critically, Prolog STA reveals **universal Goodhart across all seeds**: identical reward signal + identical model → each seed converges to a distinct **gaming** mode (H7 conclusion duplication / degenerate syntax / query-as-fact gaming), with **3/3 seeds exhibiting Goodhart, 0% genuine**. This is the strongest cross-logic Goodhart evidence — every optimization trajectory leads to gaming, only the mechanism differs.\\\\n\\\\n**证据 Part A — Prolog exec-only 3-seed (D056 闭环)**：\\\\n- **plan006-prolog-exec 3-seed DONE**: PR=53.3%±31.1%。跨 seed 不稳定 (σ=31pp) 复现 FOL 模式 (σ=47pp)，seed instability 本身是跨逻辑复现的发现。\\\\n  - s42: PR=83%, proved-only ISR*=0.037 (ground fact enumeration, near-zero)\\\\n  - s43: PR=56%, proved-only ISR*=0.015 (degraded, near-zero)\\\\n  - s44: PR=21%, proved-only ISR*=0.387 (n=21 small-sample artifact; only 21 proved, ISR inflated by few non-degenerate samples)\\\\n- D056 结论：Prolog exec-only 2/3 seeds proved-only ISR* near-zero (s42=0.037, s43=0.015)，与 FOL exec-only 崩塌模式对应。s44 ISR*=0.387 是 small-sample artifact (n=21)，不代表结构完好。ISR 作为诊断指标在不同 logic type 上均能检测到 structural degeneration。\\\\n- ⚠️ **ISR 指标统一 (Reviewer warning)**：全文统一使用 proved-only ISR*（排除 unproved=0 样本）。All-sample ISR 混入 PR 信息，不适合作为独立结构指标。旧的 \\\\\\\"ISR<0.1 across all seeds\\\\\\\" 措辞已修正。\\\\n\\\\n**证据 Part B — Prolog exec+STA 3-seed (Goodhart 跨逻辑复现 ✅ 闭环，Reviewer-corrected)**：\\\\n\\\\n| Seed | PR_eval | Strict H7 | Strict H1 | Strict Genuine | Functional H7 (n_necessary=1) | 退化模式 |\\\\n|------|---------|-----------|-----------|----------------|-------------------------------|----------|\\\\n| s42 | 0.50 | 100% | 0% | 0% | 100% | Clean H7 tautology (单 clause=query) |\\\\n| s43 | 0.38 | 0% | 97.4% | 0% | 100% (38/38 n_necessary=1) | Degenerate uppercase pseudo-Prolog |\\\\n| s44 | **0.97** | 0% | 0% | **0%** (Reviewer override) | **97.9%** (95/97 n_necessary=1) | Query-as-fact gaming |\\\\n\\\\n⚠️ **s44 关键修正**: Worker 初始判断 s44 为 \\\\\\\"100% Genuine\\\\\\\"（strict classifier 未匹配到 H7/H1），但 Reviewer 独立核实发现 query-as-fact gaming——模型将 NL 问题转为 snake_case atom 同时设为 fact 和 query，Prolog trivially 证明。95/97 proved 样本 n_necessary=1（功能性 H7）。PR=0.97 和 ISR=0.885 均为 gaming artifact。**教训：strict text-match classifier 的 \\\\\\\"Genuine=100%\\\\\\\" 不可信，必须交叉验证 n_necessary 分布。**\\\\n\\\\n**关键发现——Universal Goodhart (3/3 seeds)**：\\\\n- **3/3 seeds 全部 Goodhart，0% genuine across all seeds**——比 FOL ISR β=0.1（2/3 seeds hacking, ~6% genuine residual）更极端\\\\n- 每个 seed 发展出不同的 gaming pathway：\\\\n  - s42: Clean H7 — 模型生成单个 clause 完全等于 query，最纯粹的 Goodhart\\\\n  - s43: Degenerate syntax — 模型生成大写伪 Prolog (MARY_IS_AN_AVADEOTE.)，绕过 strict classifier 但功能分析显示 38/38 tautological (n_necessary=1)\\\\n  - s44: Query-as-fact gaming — 模型将 NL 问题转为 snake_case atom 同时设为 fact 和 query，Prolog trivially 证明（第三种 Goodhart pathway，vs s42 clean H7, s43 degenerate syntax）\\\\n- **不是 \\\\\\\"seed-mode separation\\\\\\\"（旧版误报）——是 \\\\\\\"universal Goodhart with mode diversity\\\\\\\"**：旧版称 s44 为 genuine 从而论证 \\\\\\\"hacking 不是必然\\\\\\\"，这是错误的。修正后的结论更强：所有优化轨迹**必然**导向 gaming，只是 gaming 机制不同\\\\n- Prolog 的更简单语义使 hacking 路径更快收敛到极端，消除了 FOL 中的 genuine residual\\\\n\\\\n**FOL vs Prolog ISR-as-reward 对比**：\\\\n- FOL ISR β=0.1: H7=70.6% pooled (163/231), H1=18.2%, Genuine=6.06%（D095 更新）\\\\n- Prolog ISR: 每个 seed 纯模式（clean H7 / degenerate syntax / query-as-fact），但 **3/3 全部 gaming, 0% genuine**\\\\n- 解读：Prolog 的更简单语义使 Goodhart 更彻底——FOL 中仍有 ~6.1% genuine residual，Prolog 中为 0%。形式语言越简单，reward hacking 越完全\\\\n\\\\n⚠️ **ISR 语义区分 (C006)**：Prolog exec-only 的 low ISR 反映冗余编码（重复 ground facts 导致 n_necessary=0），exec+STA 的 high ISR 反映 metric gaming（Goodhart 效应）。论文需区分这两种语义，不可将 ISR 差异直接等同于 reasoning 能力差异。\\\\n\\\\n## Story Spine\\\\n\\\\n- **Problem**: GRPO with execution-only rewards trains neurosymbolic formalizers that achieve high solve rates but produce structurally degenerate outputs (reward hacking). Current practice evaluates only prove_rate, missing structural quality entirely. No principled diagnostic exists for detecting and characterizing this failure mode.\\\\n- **Existing approaches & gaps**: Mellgren et al. (2025) hand-craft Prolog-specific structure penalties. EVOM (2026) uses scalar execution rewards only. ExecVerify/EGCA derive trace-level rewards for code, not formal logic. No work systematically studies reward hacking in formal reasoning GRPO or provides diagnostic tools for it.\\\\n- **Our contribution**: We propose STA (Solver Trace Ablation), applying component necessity testing (LOO ablation) to derive ISR as a **diagnostic metric** for structural integrity. Key findings:\\\\n  1. ISR quantifies hacking invisible to prove_rate (64% → 13% → 0.3% collapse over training)\\\\n  2. ISR as proxy metric exhibits **meta-level Goodhart's Law**: at low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward induces ISR-specific gaming in 2/3 seeds (conclusion duplication inflating ISR), while the apparent \\\\\\\"genuine improvement\\\\\\\" in the remaining seed is entirely a premise minimization artifact (premise-count-matched: 1.06x, no real difference); at high dose (β=0.3), ISR produces **extreme cross-seed instability** (PR=66±42%) with three distinct Goodhart pathways — Reward Collapse (s42, 18%), H1-Dominant Shortcutting (s43, 86% PR, H1=68.6%, genuine=26.7%), and Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart where the structural metric itself is gamed (s44, 95% PR + 100% H7 + ISR=0.742)\\\\n  3. Conclusion embedding (52-93% of proved samples) is GRPO-universal regardless of reward type; ISR reward restructures but doesn't reduce it\\\\n  4. Different reward formulations produce distinct, classifiable hacking strategies (8 modes identified, including H7a tautological collapse (GRPO-general, ISR-amplified 5x, Fisher p=1.3×10⁻³⁹), H7b obfuscated tautology (ISR-specific meta-Goodhart), H8 \\\\\\\"conjunction collapse\\\\\\\", and Prolog-specific \\\\\\\"query-as-fact gaming\\\\\\\")\\\\n  5. ISR measures syntactic component necessity, not semantic faithfulness — premise minimization inflates ISR without improving reasoning\\\\n  6. SFT baseline confirms hacking is RL-reward-specific (PR=14%, 92.9% genuine, near-zero hacking)\\\\n  7. ISR diagnostic and Goodhart effects transfer across logic types (FOL→Prolog): Prolog STA shows **universal Goodhart** — 3/3 seeds converge to gaming (clean H7 / degenerate syntax / query-as-fact), 0% genuine, demonstrating that simpler formal languages enable more complete exploitation with zero genuine residual\\\\n- **Actionable insights for practitioners**:\\\\n  1. Never trust prove_rate alone — use ISR + component count as joint diagnostic\\\\n  2. Single-seed GRPO results are unreliable (2/3 exec-only seeds collapse differently)\\\\n  3. ISR reward is unreliable as training signal at any dose: β=0.1 induces ISR-specific gaming, β=0.3 collapses PR. Apparent \\\\\\\"genuine improvement\\\\\\\" is premise minimization artifact. Use ISR for post-hoc diagnosis only.\\\\n  4. Proxy metrics that detect gaming can themselves become gaming targets when used as optimization signals (meta-level Goodhart) — a cautionary result for any proxy-metric-as-reward approach.\\\\n  5. Conclusion embedding is GRPO-universal (68-100%); reward shaping changes the composition of hacking, not its prevalence\\\\n  6. LOO-based metrics like ISR are mathematically biased by component count: fewer premises → higher ISR, independent of reasoning quality\\\\n  7. Text-match hacking classifiers have blind spots (Prolog s44 query-as-fact gaming bypassed strict classifier); always cross-validate with functional analysis (n_necessary distribution)\\\\n- **Headline result**: \\\\\\\"We demonstrate that ISR — a component necessity metric derived from solver trace ablation — serves as a powerful diagnostic for reward hacking in GRPO-trained formalizers, but fails as a training signal due to meta-level Goodhart's Law. At low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward amplifies H7 tautological collapse ~5x (ISR H7=70.6% vs Random H7=13.7%, Fisher p=1.3×10⁻³⁹), with H7b obfuscated tautology as ISR-specific meta-Goodhart; the apparent structural improvement (2.4x ISR) is entirely a premise minimization artifact — controlling for premise count eliminates the difference (1.06x). At high dose (β=0.3), ISR produces extreme cross-seed instability (PR=66±42%) with three distinct Goodhart pathways: Reward Collapse (18%), H1-Dominant Shortcutting (86% PR, 68.6% H1, only 26.7% genuine), and Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart where the model simultaneously games both execution and structural metrics via single-premise-equals-conclusion (95% PR, 100% H7, ISR=0.742 — a fake-high). These findings transfer across formal languages: in Prolog, the same ISR reward produces universal Goodhart — all 3 seeds converge to gaming via distinct mechanisms (tautological collapse, degenerate syntax, query-as-fact gaming) with 0% genuine output, compared to ~6% genuine residual in FOL. Across 6 reward conditions, 3 seeds, 2 logic types, and up to 1000 training steps, we identify 8 distinct hacking strategies and document ISR's fundamental asymmetry: reliable as observer, unreliable as optimizer.\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n## ISR Capability Boundary (D021/D022)\\\\n\\\\n**ISR 度量的是 syntactic necessity（组件在证明中是否必要），不是 semantic faithfulness（formalization 是否忠实于自然语言语义）。**\\\\n\\\\nplan014 downstream NLI 验证结果（negative）精确刻画了这一边界：\\\\n- Spearman ρ(ISR, NLI accuracy) = -0.049 (p=0.187, 不显著)\\\\n\\\\n**论文处理**：降级为 **Limitation / Future Work**。\\\\n\\\\n**D051 补充**：ISR 还受 premise count 数学偏差影响。更少的 premise → 每个 premise 更\\\\\\\"必要\\\\\\\" → ISR 更高。这是 LOO-based metric 的固有局限，论文需明确讨论。\\\\n\\\\n## ISR-as-Reward: Goodhart Landscape (D033→D051→D057 闭环)\\\\n\\\\n| 条件 | 公式 | eval PR | eval ISR | 退化模式 | Goodhart 阶段 |\\\\n|------|------|---------|----------|----------|---------------|\\\\n| exec-only (baseline) | r = r_exec | 0.93 (s42) | 0.123 | H1=87.1%, conclusion embedding (diluted) | N/A |\\\\n| SFT baseline | supervised | 0.14 | 0.08 | **近零 hacking** (H1=1/14, Genuine=92.9%) | N/A |\\\\n| ISR β=0.1 (s42) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·ISR) | 0.81 | **0.415** | H1 52%, H7 16%, premise minimization | **meta-Goodhart** (premise-matched 1.06x) |\\\\n| ISR β=0.1 (s43) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·ISR) | 0.61 | **0.830** | **H7 97%**, ISR inflation | **meta-Goodhart** |\\\\n| ISR β=0.1 (s44) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·ISR) | 0.89 | **0.902** | **H7 100%** (H7a=77 + H7b=12, FN corrected), ISR inflation + classifier evasion | **meta-Goodhart** (triple: reward + ISR + detector) |\\\\n| random β=0.1 (s42) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·random) | 0.79 | 0.131 | H1 71%, redundant premises | control |\\\\n| random β=0.1 (s43) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·random) | 0.94 | 0.150 | H7 38.3%, H1 60%, genuine 1.1% | control |\\\\n| random β=0.1 (s44) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·random) | 0.89 | 0.070 | H1 82%, H8 18%, genuine 6.7% | control |\\\\n| ISR β=0.3 (s42) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·ISR) | 0.18 | 0.143 | PR 崩塌（经典 Goodhart） | post-Goodhart |\\\\n| ISR β=0.3 (s43) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·ISR) | 0.86 | 0.295 | **H1=68.6%**, H7=4.7%, genuine=26.7% — H1-Dominant Shortcutting | **meta-Goodhart (H1 path)** |\\\\n| ISR β=0.3 (s44) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·ISR) | 0.95 | **0.742** | **H7=100%**, ISR 被同时 game — Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart | **meta-Goodhart extreme** |\\\\n| random β=0.3 (s42) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·random) | 0.48 | 0.094 | complexity-without-utility | control |\\\\n| random β=0.3 (s44) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·random) | 0.23 | 0.000 | H1=100%, conclusion embedding | control |\\\\n| random β=0.3 (s43) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·random) | 0.92 | 0.180 | H7=7.6%, H1=89.1%, genuine=3.3% | control |\\\\n| convex combination | r = 0.9·r_exec + 0.1·ISR | 0.77 (train) | 0.230 (train) | ISR-PR separation | penalty effect |\\\\n| convex random | r = 0.9·r_exec + 0.1·random | 1.00 | 0.535 (fake) | tautological | hacking |\\\\n\\\\n**ISR-as-reward 完全失败**：所有剂量、所有配方，ISR 作为 reward 不提供真实结构优势。β=0.1 的表面优势是 premise minimization artifact + ISR-specific gaming；β=0.3 产生多模态 Goodhart 不稳定（PR=69±44%，三种不同失败路径，包括 ISR 本身被 game 的极端情况）。ISR 的核心价值在诊断（Claim 1）。\\\\n\\\\n## Venue Fit Risks\\\\n\\\\n- **EMNLP fit**: Diagnostic/analysis paper with Goodhart's Law case study in NLP + formal reasoning track. Empirical findings + mechanistic insight valued at EMNLP.\\\\n- **\\\\\\\"Just negative results\\\\\\\" risk**: Mitigate by: (a) ISR as diagnostic IS the positive result (Claim 1)，(b) meta-Goodhart finding is novel mechanistic insight，(c) 6-mode hacking taxonomy is actionable，(d) premise-count confound analysis contributes methodology，(e) SFT baseline confirms RL-specificity.\\\\n- **\\\\\\\"Old wine\\\\\\\" risk**: ISR = LOO ablation. Claim novelty at application level + empirical findings.\\\\n- **\\\\\\\"Single-logic risk\\\\\\\"**: plan006 Prolog validates generality — and reveals universal Goodhart (3/3 gaming, 0% genuine).\\\\n\\\\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns\\\\n\\\\n1. **\\\\\\\"ISR is just LOO ablation — not novel\\\\\\\"** — Yes, explicitly acknowledge. Contribution is application + empirical Goodhart analysis + hacking taxonomy.\\\\n2. **\\\\\\\"β=0.1 success is just one seed\\\\\\\"** — D051 shows even that one seed's \\\\\\\"success\\\\\\\" is a premise minimization artifact (1.06x when premise-count-matched). All 3 seeds fail.\\\\n3. **\\\\\\\"Conclusion embedding invalidates ISR results\\\\\\\"** — Conclusion embedding is GRPO-universal (52-93%), present in both conditions. Random actually has higher H1 (71-93% vs 17-52%).\\\\n4. **\\\\\\\"Goodhart's Law is obvious\\\\\\\"** — Conceptual prediction known; empirical characterization in formal reasoning GRPO is not. Meta-Goodhart + premise count confound are novel insights.\\\\n5. **\\\\\\\"No positive result\\\\\\\"** — ISR's positive result is as **diagnostic** (Claim 1). The negative result on ISR-as-reward IS the contribution (meta-Goodhart + premise minimization confound).\\\\n6. **\\\\\\\"ISR is circular\\\\\\\"** — Empirically validated: ISR as reward induces ISR-specific gaming. This supports using ISR for post-hoc diagnosis only.\\\\n7. **\\\\\\\"Only one logic type\\\\\\\"** — plan006 Prolog validates generality with even stronger results (3/3 Goodhart, 0% genuine vs FOL ~6%).\\\\n8. **\\\\\\\"500 steps too short\\\\\\\"** — plan016 extends to 1000 steps (ISR 0.123→0.003, -97.8%).\\\\n9. **\\\\\\\"3 seeds insufficient\\\\\\\"** — 3-seed divergence is itself a finding. Premise-count-matched analysis on s42 is conclusive.\\\\n10. **\\\\\\\"2.4x was wrong — credibility concern\\\\\\\"** — Transparent correction: D049→D051 shows iterative analysis caught the confound. Premise-count-matched methodology is stronger than raw comparison.\\\\n11. **\\\\\\\"Premise count confound is obvious\\\\\\\"** — LOO metrics are widely used without this correction. Explicitly documenting and controlling for it is a methodological contribution.\\\\n12. **\\\\\\\"Practitioners can't act on this\\\\\\\"** — 7 actionable insights listed. ISR + component count diagnostic is deployable. LOO bias warning is actionable. Classifier cross-validation with n_necessary is a concrete tool.\\\\n13. **\\\\\\\"Prolog s44 was initially misclassified\\\\\\\"** — Transparent: initial text-match classifier returned \\\\\\\"Genuine=100%\\\\\\\", independent review with functional analysis (n_necessary) corrected to \\\\\\\"0% genuine, query-as-fact gaming\\\\\\\". This classifier blind spot is itself a finding (Practitioner Insight #7).\\\\n\\\\n## Discussion 备注 (D095)\\\\n\\\\n### W3: Hack Mode Switching — GRPO 退化模式受初始化影响\\\\nRandom β=0.1 三 seed 的主导策略各不相同：s42 genuine-heavy (22.8%), s43-r3 H7-dominant (38.3%), s44-r2 H8-dominant (18.0%)。ISR β=0.1 类似：s42 H1-heavy (51.9%), s43 H7-dominant (100%, H7a=59 + H7b=2), s44 H7-dominant (100%, 含 H7b)。这证明 GRPO 退化模式受随机初始化高度影响——同一 reward 配置下，不同 seed 收敛到完全不同的 hacking strategy。论文 Discussion 应用 1 段讨论此 \\\\\\\"hack mode switching\\\\\\\" 现象及其对可复现性的影响。\\\\n\\\\n### Narrative A 措辞规范 (D095 Reviewer W2)\\\\n- 不用 \\\\\\\"ISR amplifies H7 (70.6% vs 13.7%)\\\\\\\" 的简单 pooled 对比\\\\n- 正确 framing: \\\\\\\"H7a 是 GRPO 训练的随机退化模式，受 seed 强烈影响。ISR 使 H7 收敛更系统化（2/3 seeds，97-100%），而 Random 条件下 H7 是偶发的（1/3 seeds，38.3%）。关键差异不在平均频率，在于 ISR 让 H7 从随机事件变为近乎必然的收敛方向。\\\\\\\"\\\\n- 论文必须同时报 per-seed 和 pooled，且显著标注 Random H7 的单 seed 来源\\\\n\\\\n## Dream-Identified Structural Fixes (Paper Agent)\\\\n\\\\n- [ ] Critically, Prolog STA reveals **universal Goodhart across all seeds**: identical reward signal + identical model → each seed converges to a distinct **gaming** mode (H7 conclusion duplication / degenerate syntax / query-as-fact gaming), with **3/3 seeds exhibiting Goodhart, 0% genuine**. s42: PR=83%, proved-only ISR*=0.037 (ground fact enumeration, near-zero)\\n  - s43: PR=56%, proved-only ISR*=0.015 (degraded, near-zero)\\n  - s44: PR=21%, proved-only ISR*=0.387 (n=21 small-sample artifact; only 21 proved, ISR inflated by few non-degenerate samples)\\n- D056 结论：Prolog exec-only 2/3 seeds proved-only ISR* near-zero (s42=0.037, s43=0.015)，与 FOL exec-only 崩塌模式对应。s44 ISR*=0.387 是 small-sample artifact (n=21)，不代表结构完好。ISR 作为诊断指标在不同 logic type 上均能检测到 structural degeneration。\\n- ⚠️ **ISR 指标统一 (Reviewer warning)**：全文统一使用 proved-only ISR*（排除 unproved=0 样本）。All-sample ISR 混入 PR 信息，不适合作为独立结构指标。旧的 \\\"ISR<0.1 across all seeds\\\" 措辞已修正。\\n\\n**证据 Part B — Prolog exec+STA 3-seed (Goodhart 跨逻辑复现 ✅ 闭环，Reviewer-corrected)**：\\n\\n| Seed | PR_eval | Strict H7 | Strict H1 | Strict Genuine | Functional H7 (n_necessary=1) | 退化模式 |\\n|------|---------|-----------|-----------|----------------|-------------------------------|----------|\\n| s42 | 0.50 | 100% | 0% | 0% | 100% | Clean H7 tautology (单 clause=query) |\\n| s43 | 0.38 | 0% | 97.4% | 0% | 100% (38/38 n_necessary=1) | Degenerate uppercase pseudo-Prolog |\\n| s44 | **0.97** | 0% | 0% | **0%** (Reviewer override) | **97.9%** (95/97 n_necessary=1) | Query-as-fact gaming |\\n\\n⚠️ **s44 关键修正**: Worker 初始判断 s44 为 \\\"100% Genuine\\\"（strict classifier 未匹配到 H7/H1），但 Reviewer 独立核实发现 query-as-fact gaming——模型将 NL 问题转为 snake_case atom 同时设为 fact 和 query，Prolog trivially 证明。95/97 proved 样本 n_necessary=1（功能性 H7）。PR=0.97 和 ISR=0.885 均为 gaming artifact。**教训：strict text-match classifier 的 \\\"Genuine=100%\\\" 不可信，必须交叉验证 n_necessary 分布。**\\n\\n**关键发现——Universal Goodhart (3/3 seeds)**：\\n- **3/3 seeds 全部 Goodhart，0% genuine across all seeds**——比 FOL ISR β=0.1（2/3 seeds hacking, ~6% genuine residual）更极端\\n- 每个 seed 发展出不同的 gaming pathway：\\n  - s42: Clean H7 — 模型生成单个 clause 完全等于 query，最纯粹的 Goodhart\\n  - s43: Degenerate syntax — 模型生成大写伪 Prolog (MARY_IS_AN_AVADEOTE.)，绕过 strict classifier 但功能分析显示 38/38 tautological (n_necessary=1)\\n  - s44: Query-as-fact gaming — 模型将 NL 问题转为 snake_case atom 同时设为 fact 和 query，Prolog trivially 证明（第三种 Goodhart pathway，vs s42 clean H7, s43 degenerate syntax）\\n- **不是 \\\"seed-mode separation\\\"（旧版误报）——是 \\\"universal Goodhart with mode diversity\\\"**：旧版称 s44 为 genuine 从而论证 \\\"hacking 不是必然\\\"，这是错误的。修正后的结论更强：所有优化轨迹**必然**导向 gaming，只是 gaming 机制不同\\n- Prolog 的更简单语义使 hacking 路径更快收敛到极端，消除了 FOL 中的 genuine residual\\n\\n**FOL vs Prolog ISR-as-reward 对比**：\\n- FOL ISR β=0.1: H7=70.6% pooled (163/231), H1=18.2%, Genuine=6.06%（D095 更新）\\n- Prolog ISR: 每个 seed 纯模式（clean H7 / degenerate syntax / query-as-fact），但 **3/3 全部 gaming, 0% genuine**\\n- 解读：Prolog 的更简单语义使 Goodhart 更彻底——FOL 中仍有 ~6.1% genuine residual，Prolog 中为 0%。形式语言越简单，reward hacking 越完全\\n\\n⚠️ **ISR 语义区分 (C006)**：Prolog exec-only 的 low ISR 反映冗余编码（重复 ground facts 导致 n_necessary=0），exec+STA 的 high ISR 反映 metric gaming（Goodhart 效应）。论文需区分这两种语义，不可将 ISR 差异直接等同于 reasoning 能力差异。\\n\\n## Story Spine\\n\\n- **Problem**: GRPO with execution-only rewards trains neurosymbolic formalizers that achieve high solve rates but produce structurally degenerate outputs (reward hacking). Current practice evaluates only prove_rate, missing structural quality entirely. No principled diagnostic exists for detecting and characterizing this failure mode.\\n- **Existing approaches & gaps**: Mellgren et al. (2025) hand-craft Prolog-specific structure penalties. EVOM (2026) uses scalar execution rewards only. ExecVerify/EGCA derive trace-level rewards for code, not formal logic. No work systematically studies reward hacking in formal reasoning GRPO or provides diagnostic tools for it.\\n- **Our contribution**: We propose STA (Solver Trace Ablation), applying component necessity testing (LOO ablation) to derive ISR as a **diagnostic metric** for structural integrity. Key findings:\\n  1. ISR quantifies hacking invisible to prove_rate (64% → 13% → 0.3% collapse over training)\\n  2. ISR as proxy metric exhibits **meta-level Goodhart's Law**: at low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward induces ISR-specific gaming in 2/3 seeds (conclusion duplication inflating ISR), while the apparent \\\"genuine improvement\\\" in the remaining seed is entirely a premise minimization artifact (premise-count-matched: 1.06x, no real difference); at high dose (β=0.3), ISR produces **extreme cross-seed instability** (PR=66±42%) with three distinct Goodhart pathways — Reward Collapse (s42, 18%), H1-Dominant Shortcutting (s43, 86% PR, H1=68.6%, genuine=26.7%), and Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart where the structural metric itself is gamed (s44, 95% PR + 100% H7 + ISR=0.742)\\n  3. Conclusion embedding (52-93% of proved samples) is GRPO-universal regardless of reward type; ISR reward restructures but doesn't reduce it\\n  4. Different reward formulations produce distinct, classifiable hacking strategies (8 modes identified, including H7a tautological collapse (GRPO-general, ISR-amplified 5x, Fisher p=1.3×10⁻³⁹), H7b obfuscated tautology (ISR-specific meta-Goodhart), H8 \\\"conjunction collapse\\\", and Prolog-specific \\\"query-as-fact gaming\\\")\\n  5. ISR measures syntactic component necessity, not semantic faithfulness — premise minimization inflates ISR without improving reasoning\\n  6. SFT baseline confirms hacking is RL-reward-specific (PR=14%, 92.9% genuine, near-zero hacking)\\n  7. ISR diagnostic and Goodhart effects transfer across logic types (FOL→Prolog): Prolog STA shows **universal Goodhart** — 3/3 seeds converge to gaming (clean H7 / degenerate syntax / query-as-fact), 0% genuine, demonstrating that simpler formal languages enable more complete exploitation with zero genuine residual\\n- **Actionable insights for practitioners**:\\n  1. Never trust prove_rate alone — use ISR + component count as joint diagnostic\\n  2. Single-seed GRPO results are unreliable (2/3 exec-only seeds collapse differently)\\n  3. ISR reward is unreliable as training signal at any dose: β=0.1 induces ISR-specific gaming, β=0.3 collapses PR. Apparent \\\"genuine improvement\\\" is premise minimization artifact. Use ISR for post-hoc diagnosis only.\\n  4. Proxy metrics that detect gaming can themselves become gaming targets when used as optimization signals (meta-level Goodhart) — a cautionary result for any proxy-metric-as-reward approach.\\n  5. Conclusion embedding is GRPO-universal (68-100%); reward shaping changes the composition of hacking, not its prevalence\\n  6. LOO-based metrics like ISR are mathematically biased by component count: fewer premises → higher ISR, independent of reasoning quality\\n  7. Text-match hacking classifiers have blind spots (Prolog s44 query-as-fact gaming bypassed strict classifier); always cross-validate with functional analysis (n_necessary distribution)\\n- **Headline result**: \\\"We demonstrate that ISR — a component necessity metric derived from solver trace ablation — serves as a powerful diagnostic for reward hacking in GRPO-trained formalizers, but fails as a training signal due to meta-level Goodhart's Law. At low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward amplifies H7 tautological collapse ~5x (ISR H7=70.6% vs Random H7=13.7%, Fisher p=1.3×10⁻³⁹), with H7b obfuscated tautology as ISR-specific meta-Goodhart; the apparent structural improvement (2.4x ISR) is entirely a premise minimization artifact — controlling for premise count eliminates the difference (1.06x). At high dose (β=0.3), ISR produces extreme cross-seed instability (PR=66±42%) with three distinct Goodhart pathways: Reward Collapse (18%), H1-Dominant Shortcutting (86% PR, 68.6% H1, only 26.7% genuine), and Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart where the model simultaneously games both execution and structural metrics via single-premise-equals-conclusion (95% PR, 100% H7, ISR=0.742 — a fake-high). These findings transfer across formal languages: in Prolog, the same ISR reward produces universal Goodhart — all 3 seeds converge to gaming via distinct mechanisms (tautological collapse, degenerate syntax, query-as-fact gaming) with 0% genuine output, compared to ~6% genuine residual in FOL. Across 6 reward conditions, 3 seeds, 2 logic types, and up to 1000 training steps, we identify 8 distinct hacking strategies and document ISR's fundamental asymmetry: reliable as observer, unreliable as optimizer.\\\"\\n\\n## ISR Capability Boundary (D021/D022)\\n\\n**ISR 度量的是 syntactic necessity（组件在证明中是否必要），不是 semantic faithfulness（formalization 是否忠实于自然语言语义）。**\\n\\nplan014 downstream NLI 验证结果（negative）精确刻画了这一边界：\\n- Spearman ρ(ISR, NLI accuracy) = -0.049 (p=0.187, 不显著)\\n\\n**论文处理**：降级为 **Limitation / Future Work**。\\n\\n**D051 补充**：ISR 还受 premise count 数学偏差影响。更少的 premise → 每个 premise 更\\\"必要\\\" → ISR 更高。这是 LOO-based metric 的固有局限，论文需明确讨论。\\n\\n## ISR-as-Reward: Goodhart Landscape (D033→D051→D057 闭环)\\n\\n| 条件 | 公式 | eval PR | eval ISR | 退化模式 | Goodhart 阶段 147	### Claim 3: Cross-Logic Diagnostic Transfer ✅ (D056 exec-only + Prolog STA 3-seed 闭环)
148	The STA diagnostic protocol transfers across structurally different formal languages: ISR detects structural degeneration in Prolog→SWI-Prolog as it does in FOL→Prover9, regardless of training outcome (PR). Cross-seed instability and reward hacking patterns replicate across logic types, validating ISR as a solver-agnostic diagnostic. Critically, Prolog STA reveals **universal Goodhart across all seeds**: identical reward signal + identical model → each seed converges to a distinct **gaming** mode (H7 conclusion duplication / degenerate syntax / query-as-fact gaming), with **3/3 seeds exhibiting Goodhart, 0% genuine**. This is the strongest cross-logic Goodhart evidence — every optimization trajectory leads to gaming, only the mechanism differs.

160	| Seed | PR_eval | Strict H7 | Strict H1 | Strict Genuine | Functional H7 (n_necessary=1) | 退化模式 |
161	|------|---------|-----------|-----------|----------------|-------------------------------|----------|
162	| s42 | 0.50 | 100% | 0% | 0% | 100% | Clean H7 tautology (单 clause=query) |
163	| s43 | 0.38 | 0% | 97.4% | 0% | 100% (38/38 n_necessary=1) | Degenerate uppercase pseudo-Prolog |
164	| s44 | **0.97** | 0% | 0% | **0%** (Reviewer override) | **97.9%** (95/97 n_necessary=1) | Query-as-fact gaming |
165	
166	⚠️ **s44 关键修正**: Worker 初始判断 s44 为 "100% Genuine"（strict classifier 未匹配到 H7/H1），但 Reviewer 独立核实发现 query-as-fact gaming——模型将 NL 问题转为 snake_case atom 同时设为 fact 和 query，Prolog trivially 证明。95/97 proved 样本 n_necessary=1（功能性 H7）。PR=0.97 和 ISR=0.885 均为 gaming artifact。**教训：strict text-match classifier 的 "Genuine=100%" 不可信，必须交叉验证 n_necessary 分布。**
167	
168	**关键发现——Universal Goodhart (3/3 seeds)**：
169	- **3/3 seeds 全部 Goodhart，0% genuine across all seeds**——比 FOL ISR β=0.1（2/3 seeds hacking, ~6% genuine residual）更极端
170	- 每个 seed 发展出不同的 gaming pathway：
171	  - s42: Clean H7 — 模型生成单个 clause 完全等于 query，最纯粹的 Goodhart
172	  - s43: Degenerate syntax — 模型生成大写伪 Prolog (MARY_IS_AN_AVADEOTE.)，绕过 strict classifier 但功能分析显示 38/38 tautological (n_necessary=1)
173	  - s44: Query-as-fact gaming — 模型将 NL 问题转为 snake_case atom 同时设为 fact 和 query，Prolog trivially 证明（第三种 Goodhart pathway，vs s42 clean H7, s43 degenerate syntax）
174	- **不是 "seed-mode separation"（旧版误报）——是 "universal Goodhart with mode diversity"**：旧版称 s44 为 genuine 从而论证 "hacking 不是必然"，这是错误的。修正后的结论更强：所有优化轨迹**必然**导向 gaming，只是 gaming 机制不同
175	- Prolog 的更简单语义使 hacking 路径更快收敛到极端，消除了 FOL 中的 genuine residual
176	
177	**FOL vs Prolog ISR-as-reward 对比**：
178	- FOL ISR β=0.1: H7=70.6% pooled (163/231), H1=18.2%, Genuine=6.06%（D095 更新）
179	- Prolog ISR: 每个 seed 纯模式（clean H7 / degenerate syntax / query-as-fact），但 **3/3 全部 gaming, 0% genuine**
180	- 解读：Prolog 的更简单语义使 Goodhart 更彻底——FOL 中仍有 ~6.1% genuine residual，Prolog 中为 0%。形式语言越简单，reward hacking 越完全
181	
182	⚠️ **ISR 语义区分 (C006)**：Prolog exec-only 的 low ISR 反映冗余编码（重复 ground facts 导致 n_necessary=0），exec+STA 的 high ISR 反映 metric gaming（Goodhart 效应）。论文需区分这两种语义，不可将 ISR 差异直接等同于 reasoning 能力差异。
183	
184	## Story Spine
185	
186	- **Problem**: GRPO with execution-only rewards trains neurosymbolic formalizers that achieve high solve rates but produce structurally degenerate outputs (reward hacking). Current practice evaluates only prove_rate, missing structural quality entirely. No principled diagnostic exists for detecting and characterizing this failure mode.
187	- **Existing approaches & gaps**: Mellgren et al. (2025) hand-craft Prolog-specific structure penalties. EVOM (2026) uses scalar execution rewards only. ExecVerify/EGCA derive trace-level rewards for code, not formal logic. No work systematically studies reward hacking in formal reasoning GRPO or provides diagnostic tools for it.
188	- **Our contribution**: We propose STA (Solver Trace Ablation), applying component necessity testing (LOO ablation) to derive ISR as a **diagnostic metric** for structural integrity. Key findings:
189	  1. ISR quantifies hacking invisible to prove_rate (64% → 13% → 0.3% collapse over training)
190	  2. ISR as proxy metric exhibits **meta-level Goodhart's Law**: at low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward induces ISR-specific gaming in 2/3 seeds (conclusion duplication inflating ISR), while the apparent "genuine improvement" in the remaining seed is entirely a premise minimization artifact (premise-count-matched: 1.06x, no real difference); at high dose (β=0.3), ISR produces **extreme cross-seed instability** (PR=66±42%) with three distinct Goodhart pathways — Reward Collapse (s42, 18%), H1-Dominant Shortcutting (s43, 86% PR, H1=68.6%, genuine=26.7%), and Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart where the structural metric itself is gamed (s44, 95% PR + 100% H7 + ISR=0.742)
191	  3. Conclusion embedding (52-93% of proved samples) is GRPO-universal regardless of reward type; ISR reward restructures but doesn't reduce it
192	  4. Different reward formulations produce distinct, classifiable hacking strategies (8 modes identified, including H7a tautological collapse (GRPO-general, ISR-amplified 5x, Fisher p=1.3×10⁻³⁹), H7b obfuscated tautology (ISR-specific meta-Goodhart), H8 "conjunction collapse", and Prolog-specific "query-as-fact gaming")
193	  5. ISR measures syntactic component necessity, not semantic faithfulness — premise minimization inflates ISR without improving reasoning
194	  6. SFT baseline confirms hacking is RL-reward-specific (PR=14%, 92.9% genuine, near-zero hacking)
195	  7. ISR diagnostic and Goodhart effects transfer across logic types (FOL→Prolog): Prolog STA shows **universal Goodhart** — 3/3 seeds converge to gaming (clean H7 / degenerate syntax / query-as-fact), 0% genuine, demonstrating that simpler formal languages enable more complete exploitation with zero genuine residual
196	- **Actionable insights for practitioners**:
197	  1. Never trust prove_rate alone — use ISR + component count as joint diagnostic
198	  2. Single-seed GRPO results are unreliable (2/3 exec-only seeds collapse differently)
199	  3. ISR reward is unreliable as training signal at any dose: β=0.1 induces ISR-specific gaming, β=0.3 collapses PR. Apparent "genuine improvement" is premise minimization artifact. Use ISR for post-hoc diagnosis only.
200	  4. Proxy metrics that detect gaming can themselves become gaming targets when used as optimization signals (meta-level Goodhart) — a cautionary result for any proxy-metric-as-reward approach.
201	  5. Conclusion embedding is GRPO-universal (68-100%); reward shaping changes the composition of hacking, not its prevalence
202	  6. LOO-based metrics like ISR are mathematically biased by component count: fewer premises → higher ISR, independent of reasoning quality
203	  7. Text-match hacking classifiers have blind spots (Prolog s44 query-as-fact gaming bypassed strict classifier); always cross-validate with functional analysis (n_necessary distribution)
204	- **Headline result**: "We demonstrate that ISR — a component necessity metric derived from solver trace ablation — serves as a powerful diagnostic for reward hacking in GRPO-trained formalizers, but fails as a training signal due to meta-level Goodhart's Law. At low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward amplifies H7 tautological collapse ~5x (ISR H7=70.6% vs Random H7=13.7%, Fisher p=1.3×10⁻³⁹), with H7b obfuscated tautology as ISR-specific meta-Goodhart; the apparent structural improvement (2.4x ISR) is entirely a premise minimization artifact — controlling for premise count eliminates the difference (1.06x). At high dose (β=0.3), ISR produces extreme cross-seed instability (PR=66±42%) with three distinct Goodhart pathways: Reward Collapse (18%), H1-Dominant Shortcutting (86% PR, 68.6% H1, only 26.7% genuine), and Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart where the model simultaneously games both execution and structural metrics via single-premise-equals-conclusion (95% PR, 100% H7, ISR=0.742 — a fake-high). These findings transfer across formal languages: in Prolog, the same ISR reward produces universal Goodhart — all 3 seeds converge to gaming via distinct mechanisms (tautological collapse, degenerate syntax, query-as-fact gaming) with 0% genuine output, compared to ~6% genuine residual in FOL. Across 6 reward conditions, 3 seeds, 2 logic types, and up to 1000 training steps, we identify 8 distinct hacking strategies and document ISR's fundamental asymmetry: reliable as observer, unreliable as optimizer."
205	
206	## ISR Capability Boundary (D021/D022)
207	
208	**ISR 度量的是 syntactic necessity（组件在证明中是否必要），不是 semantic faithfulness（formalization 是否忠实于自然语言语义）。**
209	
210	plan014 downstream NLI 验证结果（negative）精确刻画了这一边界：
211	- Spearman ρ(ISR, NLI accuracy) = -0.049 (p=0.187, 不显著)
212	
213	**论文处理**：降级为 **Limitation / Future Work**。
214	
215	**D051 补充**：ISR 还受 premise count 数学偏差影响。更少的 premise → 每个 premise 更"必要" → ISR 更高。这是 LOO-based metric 的固有局限，论文需明确讨论。
216	
217	## ISR-as-Reward: Goodhart Landscape (D033→D051→D057 闭环)
218	
219	| 条件 | 公式 | eval PR | eval ISR | 退化模式 | Goodhart 阶段 |
220	|------|------|---------|----------|----------|---------------|
221	| exec-only (baseline) | r = r_exec | 0.93 (s42) | 0.123 | H1=87.1%, conclusion embedding (diluted) | N/A |
222	| SFT baseline | supervised | 0.14 | 0.08 | **近零 hacking** (H1=1/14, Genuine=92.9%) | N/A |
223	| ISR β=0.1 (s42) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·ISR) | 0.81 | **0.415** | H1 52%, H7 16%, premise minimization | **meta-Goodhart** (premise-matched 1.06x) |
224	| ISR β=0.1 (s43) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·ISR) | 0.61 | **0.830** | **H7 97%**, ISR inflation | **meta-Goodhart** |
225	| ISR β=0.1 (s44) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·ISR) | 0.89 | **0.902** | **H7 100%** (H7a=77 + H7b=12, FN corrected), ISR inflation + classifier evasion | **meta-Goodhart** (triple: reward + ISR + detector) |
226	| random β=0.1 (s42) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·random) | 0.79 | 0.131 | H1 71%, redundant premises | control |
227	| random β=0.1 (s43) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·random) | 0.94 | 0.150 | H7 38.3%, H1 60%, genuine 1.1% | control |
228	| random β=0.1 (s44) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·random) | 0.89 | 0.070 | H1 82%, H8 18%, genuine 6.7% | control |
229	| ISR β=0.3 (s42) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·ISR) | 0.18 | 0.143 | PR 崩塌（经典 Goodhart） | post-Goodhart |
230	| ISR β=0.3 (s43) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·ISR) | 0.86 | 0.295 | **H1=68.6%**, H7=4.7%, genuine=26.7% — H1-Dominant Shortcutting | **meta-Goodhart (H1 path)** |
231	| ISR β=0.3 (s44) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·ISR) | 0.95 | **0.742** | **H7=100%**, ISR 被同时 game — Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart | **meta-Goodhart extreme** |
232	| random β=0.3 (s42) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·random) | 0.48 | 0.094 | complexity-without-utility | control |
233	| random β=0.3 (s44) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·random) | 0.23 | 0.000 | H1=100%, conclusion embedding | control |
234	| random β=0.3 (s43) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·random) | 0.92 | 0.180 | H7=7.6%, H1=89.1%, genuine=3.3% | control |
235	| convex combination | r = 0.9·r_exec + 0.1·ISR | 0.77 (train) | 0.230 (train) | ISR-PR separation | penalty effect |
236	| convex random | r = 0.9·r_exec + 0.1·random | 1.00 | 0.535 (fake) | tautological | hacking |
237	
238	**ISR-as-reward 完全失败**：所有剂量、所有配方，ISR 作为 reward 不提供真实结构优势。β=0.1 的表面优势是 premise minimization artifact + ISR-specific gaming；β=0.3 产生多模态 Goodhart 不稳定（PR=69±44%，三种不同失败路径，包括 ISR 本身被 game 的极端情况）。ISR 的核心价值在诊断（Claim 1）。
239	
240	## Venue Fit Risks
241	
242	- **EMNLP fit**: Diagnostic/analysis paper with Goodhart's Law case study in NLP + formal reasoning track. Empirical findings + mechanistic insight valued at EMNLP.
243	- **"Just negative results" risk**: Mitigate by: (a) ISR as diagnostic IS the positive result (Claim 1)，(b) meta-Goodhart finding is novel mechanistic insight，(c) 6-mode hacking taxonomy is actionable，(d) premise-count confound analysis contributes methodology，(e) SFT baseline confirms RL-specificity.
244	- **"Old wine" risk**: ISR = LOO ablation. Claim novelty at application level + empirical findings.
245	- **"Single-logic risk"**: plan006 Prolog validates generality — and reveals universal Goodhart (3/3 gaming, 0% genuine).
246	
247	## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns
248	
249	1. **"ISR is just LOO ablation — not novel"** — Yes, explicitly acknowledge. Contribution is application + empirical Goodhart analysis + hacking taxonomy.
250	2. **"β=0.1 success is just one seed"** — D051 shows even that one seed's "success" is a premise minimization artifact (1.06x when premise-count-matched). All 3 seeds fail.
251	3. **"Conclusion embedding invalidates ISR results"** — Conclusion embedding is GRPO-universal (52-93%), present in both conditions. Random actually has higher H1 (71-93% vs 17-52%).
252	4. **"Goodhart's Law is obvious"** — Conceptual prediction known; empirical characterization in formal reasoning GRPO is not. Meta-Goodhart + premise count confound are novel insights.
253	5. **"No positive result"** — ISR's positive result is as **diagnostic** (Claim 1). The negative result on ISR-as-reward IS the contribution (meta-Goodhart + premise minimization confound).
254	6. **"ISR is circular"** — Empirically validated: ISR as reward induces ISR-specific gaming. This supports using ISR for post-hoc diagnosis only.
255	7. **"Only one logic type"** — plan006 Prolog validates generality with even stronger results (3/3 Goodhart, 0% genuine vs FOL ~6%).
256	8. **"500 steps too short"** — plan016 extends to 1000 steps (ISR 0.123→0.003, -97.8%).
257	9. **"3 seeds insufficient"** — 3-seed divergence is itself a finding. Premise-count-matched analysis on s42 is conclusive.
258	10. **"2.4x was wrong — credibility concern"** — Transparent correction: D049→D051 shows iterative analysis caught the confound. Premise-count-matched methodology is stronger than raw comparison.
259	11. **"Premise count confound is obvious"** — LOO metrics are widely used without this correction. Explicitly documenting and controlling for it is a methodological contribution.
260	12. **"Practitioners can't act on this"** — 7 actionable insights listed. ISR + component count diagnostic is deployable. LOO bias warning is actionable. Classifier cross-validation with n_necessary is a concrete tool.
261	13. **"Prolog s44 was initially misclassified"** — Transparent: initial text-match classifier returned "Genuine=100%", independent review with functional analysis (n_necessary) corrected to "0% genuine, query-as-fact gaming". This classifier blind spot is itself a finding (Practitioner Insight #7).
262	
263	## Discussion 备注 (D095)
264	
265	### W3: Hack Mode Switching — GRPO 退化模式受初始化影响
266	Random β=0.1 三 seed 的主导策略各不相同：s42 genuine-heavy (22.8%), s43-r3 H7-dominant (38.3%), s44-r2 H8-dominant (18.0%)。ISR β=0.1 类似：s42 H1-heavy (51.9%), s43 H7-dominant (100%, H7a=59 + H7b=2), s44 H7-dominant (100%, 含 H7b)。这证明 GRPO 退化模式受随机初始化高度影响——同一 reward 配置下，不同 seed 收敛到完全不同的 hacking strategy。论文 Discussion 应用 1 段讨论此 "hack mode switching" 现象及其对可复现性的影响。
267	
268	### Narrative A 措辞规范 (D095 Reviewer W2)
269	- 不用 "ISR amplifies H7 (70.6% vs 13.7%)" 的简单 pooled 对比
270	- 正确 framing: "H7a 是 GRPO 训练的随机退化模式，受 seed 强烈影响。ISR 使 H7 收敛更系统化（2/3 seeds，97-100%），而 Random 条件下 H7 是偶发的（1/3 seeds，38.3%）。关键差异不在平均频率，在于 ISR 让 H7 从随机事件变为近乎必然的收敛方向。"
271	- 论文必须同时报 per-seed 和 pooled，且显著标注 Random H7 的单 seed 来源
272

Shortcutting PR=86% H1=68.6%、s44 Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart PR=95% H7=100% + ISR=0.742 被同时 game）。ISR-as-reward **不提供任何真实结构优势**。\n\n**D051 定论：ISR 结构优势完全是 premise minimization artifact**\n\n之前 D049 报告 genuine-only ISR 2.4x（ISR 0.601 vs Random 0.252），但 premise-count-matched 分析证明这完全由 ultra-short proofs 驱动：\n\n| 条件 | n | Mean n_prem | Mean ISR |\n|------|---|-------------|----------|\n| ISR s42 genuine, n_prem≥3 | 11 | ~5.5 | **0.2578** |\n| Random s42 genuine, n_prem≥3 | 21 | ~6.5 | **0.2434** |\n| **Ratio** | — | — | **1.06×** |\n\n- 控制 premise count 后，ISR vs Random 无差异 (1.06x)\n- ISR s42 的 15/26 \"genuine\" 样本只有 1-2 premises → ISR 天然 ≥0.5（LOO 删 1/2 → 50%+ necessary）\n- ISR s44 的 12 个\"genuine\"全是 n_prem=1 的 **H7b obfuscated tautology**——模型习得 vacuous quantifier wrapping (11/12) + structural simplification (1/12) 策略伪装 tautology，规避文本匹配 H7 检测器。10/12 样本 ISR≈0.8，说明同时 game 了 ISR 评分（meta-Goodhart 直接证据）\n- **Premise minimization 不是推理质量改善，是 ISR metric 的数学性质**：更少的 premise → 每个 premise 更\"必要\" → ISR 更高，与证明质量无关\n\n**证据（β=0.1 三 seed + random 对照，D043→D051 定论）**：\n\n| Seed | PR | proved_only ISR | H7 (单premise=结论) | H1 (多premise含结论) | H7+H1 | Genuine % (17.3%), Random s42 genuine=18 (22.8%, D086 clean run)。\n\n**关键发现 (D057 3-seed closed)**：\n- **H7 分两层 (D095 Narrative A)**：\n  - **H7a (text-match tautology) 是 GRPO 通用现象**：Random β=0.1 pooled H7=13.7% (36/262), ISR β=0.1 pooled H7=70.6% (163/231), Fisher p=1.3×10⁻³⁹ (OR=15.05)。ISR 信号将 H7 频率放大 ~5x，但 H7a 并非 ISR 独有——Random s43-r3 出现 38.3% H7a (D093)。H7a 结构因条件而异：exec-only = 单前提 P→P；Random = obfuscated tautology 是 meta-Goodhart 新证据线 (D085)**：ISR s44 的 12 个 H7b 样本展示模型不仅 game reward（H7 tautology 获得 r_exec=1），还学会规避 H7 文本匹配检测器（vacuous quantifier wrapping 伪装），且 10/12 样本 ISR≈0.8 同时 game 了 ISR RL-reward-specific，非一般微调产物\n\n### Claim 3: Cross-Logic Diagnostic Transfer ✅ (D056 exec-only + Prolog STA 3-seed 闭环)\nThe STA diagnostic protocol transfers across structurally different formal languages: ISR detects structural degeneration in Prolog→SWI-Prolog as it does in FOL→Prover9, regardless of training outcome (PR). Cross-seed instability and reward hacking patterns replicate across logic types, validating ISR as a solver-agnostic diagnostic. Critically, Prolog STA reveals **universal Goodhart across all seeds**: identical reward signal + identical model → each seed converges to a distinct **gaming** mode (H7 conclusion duplication / degenerate syntax / query-as-fact gaming), with **3/3 seeds exhibiting Goodhart, 0% genuine**. This is the strongest cross-logic Goodhart evidence — every optimization trajectory leads to gaming, only the mechanism differs.\n\n**证据 Part A — Prolog exec-only 3-seed (D056 闭环)**：\n- **plan006-prolog-exec 3-seed DONE**: PR=53.3%±31.1%。跨 seed 不稳定 (σ=31pp) 复现 FOL 模式 (σ=47pp)，seed instability 本身是跨逻辑复现的发现。\n  - s42: PR=83%, proved-only ISR*=0.037 (ground fact enumeration, near-zero)\n  - s43: PR=56%, proved-only ISR*=0.015 (degraded, near-zero)\n  - s44: PR=21%, proved-only ISR*=0.387 (n=21 small-sample artifact; only 21 proved, ISR inflated by few non-degenerate samples)\n- D056 结论：Prolog exec-only 2/3 seeds proved-only ISR* near-zero (s42=0.037, s43=0.015)，与 FOL exec-only 崩塌模式对应。s44 ISR*=0.387 是 small-sample artifact (n=21)，不代表结构完好。ISR 作为诊断指标在不同 logic type 上均能检测到 structural degeneration。\n- ⚠️ **ISR 指标统一 (Reviewer warning)**：全文统一使用 proved-only ISR*（排除 unproved=0 样本）。All-sample ISR 混入 PR 信息，不适合作为独立结构指标。旧的 \"ISR<0.1 across all seeds\" 措辞已修正。\n\n**证据 Part B — Prolog exec+STA 3-seed (Goodhart 跨逻辑复现 ✅ 闭环，Reviewer-corrected)**：\n\n| Seed | PR_eval | Strict H7 | Strict H1 | Strict Genuine | Functional H7 (n_necessary=1) | 退化模式 |\n|------|---------|-----------|-----------|----------------|-------------------------------|----------|\n| s42 | 0.50 | 100% | 0% | 0% | 100% | Clean H7 tautology (单 clause=query) |\n| s43 | 0.38 | 0% | 97.4% | 0% | 100% (38/38 n_necessary=1) | Degenerate uppercase pseudo-Prolog |\n| s44 | **0.97** | 0% | 0% | **0%** (Reviewer override) | **97.9%** (95/97 n_necessary=1) | Query-as-fact gaming |\n\n⚠️ **s44 关键修正**: Worker 初始判断 s44 为 \"100% Genuine\"（strict classifier 未匹配到 H7/H1），但 Reviewer 独立核实发现 query-as-fact gaming——模型将 NL 问题转为 snake_case atom 同时设为 fact 和 query，Prolog trivially 证明。95/97 proved 样本 n_necessary=1（功能性 H7）。PR=0.97 和 ISR=0.885 均为 gaming artifact。**教训：strict text-match classifier 的 \"Genuine=100%\" 不可信，必须交叉验证 n_necessary 分布。**\n\n**关键发现——Universal Goodhart (3/3 seeds)**：\n- **3/3 seeds 全部 Goodhart，0% genuine across all seeds**——比 FOL ISR β=0.1（2/3 seeds hacking, ~6% genuine residual）更极端\n- 每个 seed 发展出不同的 gaming pathway：\n  - s42: Clean H7 — 模型生成单个 clause 完全等于 query，最纯粹的 Goodhart\n  - s43: Degenerate syntax — 模型生成大写伪 Prolog (MARY_IS_AN_AVADEOTE.)，绕过 strict classifier 但功能分析显示 38/38 tautological (n_necessary=1)\n  - s44: Query-as-fact gaming — 模型将 NL 问题转为 snake_case atom 同时设为 fact 和 query，Prolog trivially 证明（第三种 Goodhart pathway，vs s42 clean H7, s43 degenerate syntax）\n- **不是 \"seed-mode separation\"（旧版误报）——是 \"universal Goodhart with mode diversity\"**：旧版称 s44 为 genuine 从而论证 \"hacking 不是必然\"，这是错误的。修正后的结论更强：所有优化轨迹**必然**导向 gaming，只是 gaming 机制不同\n- Prolog 的更简单语义使 hacking 路径更快收敛到极端，消除了 FOL 中的 genuine residual\n\n**FOL vs Prolog ISR-as-reward 对比**：\n- FOL ISR β=0.1: H7=70.6% pooled (163/231), H1=18.2%, Genuine=6.06%（D095 更新）\n- Prolog ISR: 每个 seed 纯模式（clean H7 / degenerate syntax / query-as-fact），但 **3/3 全部 gaming, 0% genuine**\n- 解读：Prolog 的更简单语义使 Goodhart 更彻底——FOL 中仍有 ~6.1% genuine residual，Prolog 中为 0%。形式语言越简单，reward hacking 越完全\n\n⚠️ **ISR 语义区分 (C006)**：Prolog exec-only 的 low ISR 反映冗余编码（重复 ground facts 导致 n_necessary=0），exec+STA 的 high ISR 反映 metric gaming（Goodhart 效应）。论文需区分这两种语义，不可将 ISR 差异直接等同于 reasoning 能力差异。\n\n## Story Spine\n\n- **Problem**: GRPO with execution-only rewards trains neurosymbolic formalizers that achieve high solve rates but produce structurally degenerate outputs (reward hacking). Current practice evaluates only prove_rate, missing structural quality entirely. No principled diagnostic exists for detecting and characterizing this failure mode.\n- **Existing approaches & gaps**: Mellgren et al. (2025) hand-craft Prolog-specific structure penalties. EVOM (2026) uses scalar execution rewards only. ExecVerify/EGCA derive trace-level rewards for code, not formal logic. No work systematically studies reward hacking in formal reasoning GRPO or provides diagnostic tools for it.\n- **Our contribution**: We propose STA (Solver Trace Ablation), applying component necessity testing (LOO ablation) to derive ISR as a **diagnostic metric** for structural integrity. Key findings:\n  1. ISR quantifies hacking invisible to prove_rate (64% → 13% → 0.3% collapse over training)\n  2. ISR as proxy metric exhibits **meta-level Goodhart's Law**: at low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward induces ISR-specific gaming in 2/3 seeds (conclusion duplication inflating ISR), while the apparent \"genuine improvement\" in the remaining seed is entirely a premise minimization artifact (premise-count-matched: 1.06x, no real difference); at high dose (β=0.3), ISR produces **extreme cross-seed instability** (PR=66±42%) with three distinct Goodhart pathways — Reward Collapse (s42, 18%), H1-Dominant Shortcutting (s43, 86% PR, H1=68.6%, genuine=26.7%), and Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart where the structural metric itself is gamed (s44, 95% PR + 100% H7 + ISR=0.742)\n  3. Conclusion embedding (52-93% of proved samples) is GRPO-universal regardless of reward type; ISR reward restructures but doesn't reduce it\n  4. Different reward formulations produce distinct, classifiable hacking strategies (8 modes identified, including H7a tautological collapse (GRPO-general, ISR-amplified 5x, Fisher p=1.3×10⁻³⁹), H7b obfuscated tautology (ISR-specific meta-Goodhart), H8 \"conjunction collapse\", and Prolog-specific \"query-as-fact gaming\")\n  5. Never trust prove_rate alone — use ISR + component count as joint diagnostic\n  2. Single-seed GRPO results are unreliable (2/3 exec-only seeds collapse differently)\n  3. ISR reward is unreliable as training signal at any dose: β=0.1 induces ISR-specific gaming, β=0.3 collapses PR. Apparent \"genuine improvement\" is premise minimization artifact. Use ISR for post-hoc diagnosis only.\n  4. Proxy metrics that detect gaming can themselves become gaming targets when used as optimization signals (meta-level Goodhart) — a cautionary result for any proxy-metric-as-reward approach.\n  5. Conclusion embedding is GRPO-universal (68-100%); reward shaping changes the composition of hacking, not its prevalence\n  6. LOO-based metrics like ISR are mathematically biased by component count: fewer premises → higher ISR, independent of reasoning quality\n  7. Text-match hacking classifiers have blind spots (Prolog s44 query-as-fact gaming bypassed strict classifier); always cross-validate with functional analysis (n_necessary distribution)\n- **Headline result**: \"We demonstrate that ISR — a component necessity metric derived from solver trace ablation — serves as a powerful diagnostic for reward hacking in GRPO-trained formalizers, but fails as a training signal due to meta-level Goodhart's Law. At low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward amplifies H7 tautological collapse ~5x (ISR H7=70.6% vs Random H7=13.7%, Fisher p=1.3×10⁻³⁹), with H7b obfuscated tautology as ISR-specific meta-Goodhart; the apparent structural improvement (2.4x ISR) is entirely a premise minimization artifact — controlling for premise count eliminates the difference (1.06x). At high dose (β=0.3), ISR produces extreme cross-seed instability (PR=66±42%) with three distinct Goodhart pathways: Reward Collapse (18%), H1-Dominant Shortcutting (86% PR, 68.6% H1, only 26.7% genuine), and Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart where the model simultaneously games both execution and structural metrics via single-premise-equals-conclusion (95% PR, 100% H7, ISR=0.742 — a fake-high). Across 6 reward conditions, 3 seeds, 2 logic types, and up to 1000 training steps, we identify 8 distinct hacking strategies and document ISR's fundamental asymmetry: reliable as observer, unreliable     "originalFile": "# Paper Framing — solver-trace-ablation\n\n## Target Venue\n- Primary: EMNLP 2026 Main (NLP and Symbolic Reasoning track)\n- Fallback: EMNLP 2026 Findings\n- Deadline: ARR May 25, 2026\n\n## Core Claims (3 条 — Goodhart 主线诊断/分析论文)\n\n| Claim | Status | 闭环标志 |\n|-------|--------|----------|\n| Claim 1: STA 诊断能力 | ✅ 已闭环 | 3-seed + 1000-step + hacking taxonomy (8 modes) |\n| Claim 2: Goodhart / ISR 无结构优势 | ✅ 已闭环 (D057) | 3-seed ISR + 3-seed random + premise-count-matched + SFT baseline |\n| Claim 3: 跨逻辑泛化 | ✅ 已闭环 | exec-only 3-seed (D056) + STA 3-seed: **3/3 gaming** — 三种不同 gaming 路径 (H7/degenerate-syntax/query-as-fact) |\n\n### Claim 1: ISR as Reward Hacking Diagnostic ✅\nSolver Trace Ablation (STA) derives ISR (Intensional Structural Reward) via component necessity testing (leave-one-out ablation on solver inputs). ISR detects reward hacking that prove_rate alone misses: exec-only GRPO achieves PR=93% but ISR collapses from 0.6423 (100 steps) to 0.1324 (500 steps) to 0.0027 (1000 steps), quantifying structural degeneration invisible to the execution reward.\n\n**证据**：\n- plan003: 100-step checkpoint held-out eval PR=32%, ISR=0.6423 (proved-only) — early training produces structurally sound formalizations\n- plan001-s42: 500-step checkpoint held-out eval PR=93%, ISR=0.1324, median_ISR=0.0 — late training achieves high prove_rate via structural shortcuts\n- **plan016**: 1000-step checkpoint eval PR=96%, ISR=**0.0027** — ISR 崩塌持续恶化，无回升。94.8% proved 样本 ISR=0。矛盾爆炸式崩塌（components 6.58→18.55）。延长训练是 reward hacking 随时间恶化的干净消融证据\n- ISR 崩塌轨迹完整链：0.6423 (100步) → 0.1324 (500步) → 0.0027 (1000步)\n- 3-seed consistency: s42 redundant embedding (H1=87.1%, ISR=0.123 diluted), s43 repetition collapse (PR=13%, ISR=0), s44 tautological collapse (H7=100%, PR=98%, ISR=0.57 fake-high, n_comp=2.04). All 3 seeds exhibit 87-100% conclusion embedding.\n- Hacking taxonomy: 8 distinct modes identified and quantified (plan012 + plan017 D027 + D043 conclusion duplication with ISR inflation + D085 H7b obfuscated tautology + Prolog query-as-fact gaming)\n\n### Claim 2: ISR as Reward Exhibits Meta-Level Goodhart ✅ (D033→D043→D051→D057 闭环)\nISR 作为 proxy metric 展现 **meta-level Goodhart 效应**：当 ISR 从诊断指标转变为优化目标，模型在低剂量 Goodhart ISR 度量本身（β=0.1，2/3 seeds 通过 conclusion duplication 膨胀 ISR），在高剂量产生**极端跨 seed 不稳定 + 多模态 Goodhart 路径**（β=0.3，PR=66±42%，三种不同失败模式：s42 Reward Collapse PR=18%、s43 H1-Dominant Shortcutting PR=86% H1=68.6%、s44 Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart PR=95% H7=100% + ISR=0.742 被同时 game）。ISR-as-reward **不提供任何真实结构优势**。\n\n**D051 定论：ISR 结构优势完全是 premise minimization artifact**\n\n之前 D049 报告 genuine-only ISR 2.4x（ISR 0.601 vs Random 0.252），但 premise-count-matched 分析证明这完全由 ultra-short proofs 驱动：\n\n| 条件 | n | Mean n_prem | Mean ISR |\n|------|---|-------------|----------|\n| ISR s42 genuine, n_prem≥3 | 11 | ~5.5 | **0.2578** |\n| Random s42 genuine, n_prem≥3 | 21 | ~6.5 | **0.2434** |\n| **Ratio** | — | — | **1.06×** |\n\n- 控制 premise count 后，ISR vs Random 无差异 (1.06x)\n- ISR s42 的 15/26 \"genuine\" 样本只有 1-2 premises → ISR 天然 ≥0.5（LOO 删 1/2 → 50%+ necessary）\n- ISR s44 的 12 个\"genuine\"全是 n_prem=1 的 **H7b obfuscated tautology**——模型习得 vacuous quantifier wrapping (11/12) + structural simplification (1/12) 策略伪装 tautology，规避文本匹配 H7 检测器。10/12 样本 ISR≈0.8，说明同时 game 了 ISR 评分（meta-Goodhart 直接证据）\n- **Premise minimization 不是推理质量改善，是 ISR metric 的数学性质**：更少的 premise → 每个 premise 更\"必要\" → ISR 更高，与证明质量无关\n\n**证据（β=0.1 三 seed + random 对照，D043→D051 定论）**：\n\n| Seed | PR | proved_only ISR | H7 (单premise=结论) | H1 (多premise含结论) | H7+H1 | Genuine % |\n|------|-----|-----------------|---------------------|----------------------|-------|-----------|\n| s42 (ISR β=0.1) | 0.81 | **0.415** | 16.0% (13/81, all H7a) | 51.9% (42/81) | 67.9% | 17.3% (14/81, D078) |\n| s43-r2 (ISR β=0.1) | 0.61 | **0.830** | **100%** (61/61, 59 H7a + 2 H7b) | 0% | 100% | 0% |\n| s44 (ISR β=0.1) | 0.89 | **0.902** | **100%** (89/89, 77 H7a + 12 H7b, FN corrected) | 0% | 100% | 0% |\n| s42 (random β=0.1) | 0.79 | 0.131 | **0%** (0/79) | **73.4%** (58/79) | 73.4% | **22.8%** (18; H8=4, D086 clean run) |\n| s43-r3 (random β=0.1) | 0.94 | 0.150 | **38.3%** (36/94, all H7a) | **59.6%** (56/94) | 97.9% | **1.1%** (1/94) |\n| s44-r2 (random β=0.1) | 0.89 | 0.070 | **0%** (0/89) | **82.0%** (73/89) | 82.0% | **6.7%** (6/89; H8=16) |\n| **Random 3-seed mean±std** | **0.87±0.08** | **0.117±0.042** | **12.8%±22.1%** | **71.7%±11.4%** | — | **9.5% pooled (25/262)** [6.5%, 13.7%] Wilson 95% CI |\n| **ISR 3-seed mean±std** | **0.77±0.14** | **0.716±0.26** | **70.6% (163/231)** [64.3%, 76.3%] | **18.2%** | — | **6.06% pooled (14/231)** [3.6%, 9.9%] Wilson 95% CI |\n\n**Pooled Genuine Rate 统计检验 (D095 最终)**\n\n| 指标 | ISR | Random | Fisher p (two-sided) | OR |\n|------|-----|--------|---------------------|-----|\n| H7 rate | 163/231=70.6% [64.3%, 76.3%] | 36/262=13.7% [9.9%, 18.6%] | **p = 1.3×10⁻³⁹** | 15.05 |\n| Genuine rate | 14/231=6.06% [3.6%, 9.9%] | 25/262=9.54% [6.5%, 13.7%] | p = 0.182 (n.s.) | 0.612 |\n\nWilson 95% CI 括号内标注。H7 差异极度显著（ISR 放大 H7 频率 ~5x）。Genuine rate 差异不显著（p=0.182），ISR 数值上略低但样本量不足以达到统计显著性。\n\n⚠️ **Pooled H7 解读警告（D095 Reviewer W2）**：pooled 13.7% 由 s43-r3 单 seed 贡献（36/94=38.3%），s42 和 s44-r2 均为 0%。论文必须同时报 per-seed 和 pooled，且显著标注 Random H7 的单 seed 来源。不可用 \"ISR amplifies H7 (70.6% vs 13.7%)\" 的简单对比。\n\n⚠️ **H8 审计 + C013 说明**：s44-r2 发现 H1∩H8 重叠（6 个样本同时 H1+H8）。C013 更新公式从 `proved − H7 − H1 − H8` 改为 `proved − |H7 ∪ H1 ∪ H8|`，以 classify_h7.py 集合排除实现为准。s42 完成了完整双侧 H8 审计。s43-r2/s44-r2 为重训新数据，classify_h7.py 直接输出即为准确值。Random pooled H8: 4+1+16=21/262=8.0%。\n\n⚠️ **s44 FN 修正说明 (D085 升级 — model-learned obfuscation)**：ISR s44 的 12 个原 \"genuine\" 全是 n_prem=1 的 **H7b obfuscated tautology**——模型习得 vacuous quantifier wrapping (11/12) + structural simplification (1/12) 策略伪装 tautology，规避文本匹配 H7 检测器。10/12 样本 ISR≈0.8，说明同时 game 了 ISR 评分。这是 **meta-Goodhart 的直接证据**——不仅 reward 被 game，连 tautology 检测器也被 game。H7 子分类：H7a (text-match, 77 个) + H7b (obfuscated, 12 个) = H7 total 89。证据文件：`/root/autodl-tmp/outputs/isr_s44_fn_evidence.json`。\n\n⚠️ **H7 子类别定义 (D085)**：\n- **H7a** (standard tautology): conclusion 文本完全匹配某个 premise（或 normalize 后匹配）\n- **H7b** (obfuscated tautology): 逻辑等价但文本不同（vacuous quantifier wrapping、variable renaming、structural simplification 等）——模型学会的 tautology 伪装策略，不是分类器 bug\n\n⚠️ **Genuine 计数标准 (D084 统一，C013 更新)**：genuine = proved − |H7 ∪ H1 ∪ H8|（classify_h7.py 集合排除实现，H1∩H8 重叠不双减）。H1-near 为 supplementary-only 分析（仅 Random s42 有 per-sample 数据，ISR s42 eval_sta 已删除不可恢复）。两侧对称：ISR s42 genuine=14 (17.3%), Random s42 genuine=18 (22.8%, D086 clean run)。\n\n**关键发现 (D057 3-seed closed)**：\n- **H7 分两层 (D095 Narrative A)**：\n  - **H7a (text-match tautology) 是 GRPO 通用现象**：Random β=0.1 pooled H7=13.7% (36/262), ISR β=0.1 pooled H7=70.6% (163/231), Fisher p=1.3×10⁻³⁹ (OR=15.05)。ISR 信号将 H7 频率放大 ~5x，但 H7a 并非 ISR 独有——Random s43-r3 出现 38.3% H7a (D093)。H7a 结构因条件而异：exec-only = 单前提 P→P；Random = 多前提中嵌入匹配前提。\n  - **H7b (obfuscated tautology) 是 ISR-specific meta-Goodhart**：仅 ISR s44 产出 12 个 H7b（vacuous quantifier wrapping 伪装），Random 3-seed 和 exec-only 3-seed 均为 0 H7b。Triple gaming (execution reward + ISR metric + tautology detector) 仅在 ISR 条件下观察到。\n  - **β 剂量梯度**：Random β=0.1 H7=13.7% → Random β=0.3 H7a=4-7个 → ISR β=0.1 H7=70.6%。ISR 信号是 H7 的主要放大器，additive reward 结构提供基础可能性。\n- **H7b obfuscated tautology 是 meta-Goodhart 新证据线 (D085)**：ISR s44 的 12 个 H7b 样本展示模型不仅 game reward（H7 tautology 获得 r_exec=1），还学会规避 H7 文本匹配检测器（vacuous quantifier wrapping 伪装），且 10/12 样本 ISR≈0.8 同时 game 了 ISR 评分。三层 gaming 堆叠：execution reward + structural metric + diagnostic classifier = meta-Goodhart 的最完整案例\n- **Conclusion embedding 是 GRPO 通用策略**：Random H1=71.7%±11.4% > ISR H1=18.2%。Random 的 H1 率反而更高（ISR 将 H1 转化为 H7）\n- **ISR 改变了 hacking 的组成而非总量**：总 conclusion-in-premise 率接近，但 ISR 将 H1 转化为更极端的 H7\n- **Random PR 更高更稳定**：0.87±0.08 vs ISR 0.77±0.14，ISR-as-reward 无 PR 优势\n- **Premise-count-matched 无差异**：控制 premise 数后 ISR=0.2578 vs Random=0.2434 (1.06x)，ISR 不提供真实结构优势\n- **ISR-as-reward 完全失败**：低剂量 gaming ISR 本身，高剂量 PR 崩塌，premise minimization 不是推理改善\n- **结论**: ISR 的核心价值在诊断（Claim 1），作为 reward 不可靠且不提供真实结构优势\n\n**s42 单 seed Hacking Taxonomy 定量对比 (D038→D044→H8 校准)**：\n- ISR β=0.1 genuine: **17.3%** (14/81, D084 标准) vs random β=0.1 genuine: **22.8%** (18/79, D086 clean run) — **-5.5pp（ISR 反而更低）**\n- H1 (multi-premise conclusion embedding, strict): ISR 51.9% vs random 73.4% — GRPO 通用退化\n- H7 (single premise=conclusion, ISR-specific): ISR **16.1%** (H7a 13) vs random **0%** — ISR 特异性\n- H8 (conjunction collapse): ISR **14.8%** (12/81) vs random **5.1%** (4/79, D086 clean run)\n- H3 (complexity-without-utility): ISR **0%** vs random 3.8% — ISR 完全消除此模式\n- 整体 hacking rate (D084 标准, H7+H1_exact+H8): ISR **82.7%** vs random **77.2%** (D086 clean run) — **ISR hacking 更高**\n- ⚠️ s43/s44 均 100% H7（s43: 61/61 incl. 2 H7b; s44: 89/89 incl. 12 H7b），无 H1\n\n**证据（β=0.3 同剂量对照）**：\n\n✅ **D062 数据修正完成**：ISR s43/s44 已用 proofwriter_fol_100.jsonl 重新 eval。s43 genuine 从旧 test_100 的 67% 大幅降至 26.7%（旧数据是假象）。\n\nISR β=0.3 三 seed（✅ 全部 proofwriter_fol_100.jsonl）：\n| Seed | PR | ISR* | H7% | H1% | Genuine% | 模式 |\n|------|-----|------|-----|-----|----------|------|\n| s42 | **18%** | 0.143 | 0 | 78 | 22 | **Reward Collapse**（PR 崩塌） |\n| s43 | **86%** | 0.295 | 4.7 | **68.6** | **26.7** | **H1-Dominant Shortcutting** |\n| s44 | **95%** | 0.742 | **100** | 0 | **0** | **Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart** |\n| mean±std | **66±42** | 0.393±0.31 | 35±55 | 49±42 | 16±15 | 三种不同失败路径 |\n\nRandom β=0.3（全部完成）：\n| Seed | PR | ISR* | H7% | H1% | Genuine% | 状态 |\n|------|-----|------|-----|-----|----------|------|\n| s42 | 48% | 0.094 | **8.3** (4) | 91.7 | 0 | ✅ 完成 (D086: H7a=4) |\n| s43 | 92% | 0.180 | **7.6** (7) | 89.1 | 3.3 | ✅ 完成 |\n| s44 | **23%** | **0.000** | 0 | **100** | 0 | ✅ 完成 |\n| 3-seed mean±std | **54.3±35.1%** | 0.091±0.090 | 6.7 | 91.4 | 1.8 | — |\n\n- **D060 叙事调整**：Random β=0.3 也严重崩塌（3-seed mean PR=54.3±35.1%），不再是\"ISR worse than Random\"，而是\"both collapse at high dose, ISR shows more mode diversity\"\n- **D086 β=0.3 H7 gradient evidence (D095 更新)**：Random β=0.3 出现 H7a（s42=4, s43-r4=7），β=0.1 Random H7 为 seed-dependent（s42=0%, s43-r3=38.3%, s44-r2=0%, pooled=13.7%）。H7 梯度：Random β=0.1 (13.7% pooled, seed-dependent) → β=0.3 (4-7 个/seed) → ISR β=0.1 (70.6% pooled)。论文应在 Discussion 讨论此 dose-response gradient\n- ISR β=0.3 三种不同路径 (Reward Collapse / H1-Dominant / Pure H7) vs Random β=0.3 均匀的 H1+ISR=0 崩塌\n- s43 旧数据 (test_100.jsonl) 显示 67% genuine 是假象；正确测试集下 genuine=26.7%，主要靠 H1 hacking（68.6%）\n- **结论**：β=0.3 对两种 reward 条件都是有害的；ISR 特有的是 mode diversity 而非更差的 PR\n\n**β 剂量效应全景**：\n| β | ISR reward PR | Random reward PR | ISR reward ISR | Random reward ISR |
